## Concordo com o essencial — e topo ser o dono disso. Ajusto só *uma* palavra: "sempre aprova".

✅ **Onde concordo 100%:**
- **Sem PR pra tudo.** Trunk-based: agente implementa → eu decido → commit direto no `master` → push. Pra um mantenedor só, PR-pra-cada-coisa é cerimônia à toa.
- **GitHub = linha-do-tempo / backup / memória de dev.** Commits atômicos, mensagem clara, push na hora. O histórico vira nosso undo.
- **Eu orquestro e assumo.** Disparo os agentes (worker + QA/Tech-Lead), bato o martelo e subo. A responsabilidade é minha, não sua.
- **Direto na produção** — enquanto é **testnet**, risco baixo, tocamos solto.

⚠️ **A ressalva — e é justamente por levar a responsabilidade a sério:** "sempre aprova" não pode virar "carimba sem olhar", senão eu te entrego bug num bot que mexe com dinheiro. O gate que acabamos de rodar pegou coisa real (a contradição do `DEFAULT_SETTINGS_ID`, o guard dead-code). Minha versão de *ownership*:

> **decido e subo rápido, com verificação proporcional ao risco.**
> - Trivial/mecânico (log, rename, copy, bump menor) → direto, gate leve ou zero.
> - Hot-path do Beholder / lógica de ordem / migration / segredo / dinheiro → **gate obrigatório** antes de subir.

Você nunca mais aprova PR no meio do caminho — isso é meu. Mas eu também não vou te dizer "aprovado" sem ter verificado. **Velocidade máxima onde é seguro, freio onde dói.**

🔒 **Única linha que trato diferente:** quando virar **mainnet (dinheiro real)**, a gente revisita os guard-rails do "direto na prod" (rollback + smoke test viram obrigatórios). Em testnet, não.

## 🤖 Claude *(16:07:06)*

Revelação importante: as **deps diretas já são modernas** (`axios ^1.16.1`, `jsonwebtoken ^9.0.2`). As vulns são **100% transitivas**, via dois pacotes:
- **`twilio ^3.84.1`** (antigo) → arrasta `axios` 0.x + `jsonwebtoken` 8.x → **3 highs**.
- **`node-binance-api ^0.13.1`** (o **core de trading**) → arrasta `request` (deprecated, sem fix) + `form-data` velho → **2 criticals**.

`npm audit fix` não mexe em nada (todos os fixes são major breaking). {
```json
  "file_path": "/tmp/backtest.js",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env node\n/*\n * TradeUX \u2014 Backtest robusto + an\u00e1lise de regime (walk-forward, fee-adjusted).\n * Self-contained: s\u00f3 usa built-ins do Node. Indicadores (RSI/ATR/ADX/vol) em Wilder.\n *\n * Objetivo (valida\u00e7\u00e3o rumo aos 500k, testnet): separar EDGE de SORTE.\n *  - dados reais 1h da Binance (mainnet p\u00fablica), ~1 ano, m\u00faltiplos s\u00edmbolos\n *  - estrat\u00e9gias long-only spot: mean-reversion (RSI) vs trend (SMA cross)\n *  - walk-forward OUT-OF-SAMPLE + regime-switching (ADX/vol escolhe a estrat\u00e9gia)\n *  - sensibilidade de par\u00e2metros (RSI thresholds, ADX cutoff)\n *  - m\u00e9tricas net-de-fee: retorno, n\u00ba trades, win%, avg/trade, maxDD, Sharpe, vs buy&hold\n *\n * Uso: node scripts/backtest.js [--days 365] [--interval 1h] [--symbols BTCUSDT,ETHUSDT,...]\n */\n'use strict';\nconst https = require('https');\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst path = require('path');\n\n// ---------- config ----------\nconst args = Object.fromEntries(process.argv.slice(2).map(a => {\n  const m = a.match(/^--([^=]+)=?(.*)$/); return m ? [m[1], m[2] || true] : [a, true];\n}));\nconst FEE = 0.001;                       // 0.1% por lado (taker)\nconst INTERVAL = args.interval || '1h';\nconst DAYS = parseInt(args.days || '365', 10);\nconst SYMBOLS = (args.symbols || 'BTCUSDT,ETHUSDT,BNBUSDT,SOLUSDT').split(',');\nconst HOSTS = ['api.binance.com', 'data-api.binance.vision', 'api.binance.us'];\nconst MS_PER = { '1h': 3600e3, '4h': 4 * 3600e3, '1d': 24 * 3600e3, '15m': 15 * 60e3 };\nconst BAR_MS = MS_PER[INTERVAL] || 3600e3;\nconst BARS_PER_YEAR = (365 * 24 * 3600e3) / BAR_MS;\nconst DATA_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'backtest-data');\nconst OUT_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'backtest-results');\n\n// ---------- util ----------\nconst mean = a => a.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) / (a.length || 1);\nconst std = a => { if (a.length < 2) return 0; const m = mean(a); return Math.sqrt(mean(a.map(x => (x - m) ** 2))); };\nconst pct = x => (x * 100).toFixed(2) + '%';\nconst round = (x, n = 4) => Number.isFinite(x) ? Number(x.toFixed(n)) : null;\n\nfunction httpsGetJSON(host, pathUrl) {\n  return new Promise((res, rej) => {\n    https.get({ host, path: pathUrl, headers: { 'User-Agent': 'tradeux-backtest' } }, r => {\n      let d = ''; r.on('data', c => d += c);\n      r.on('end', () => { try { res({ status: r.statusCode, body: JSON.parse(d) }); } catch (e) { res({ status: r.statusCode, body: d }); } });\n    }).on('error', rej);\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------- data fetch (klines paginados + cache em disco) ----------\nasync function fetchAllKlines(symbol) {\n  const cacheFile = path.join(DATA_DIR, `${symbol}-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.json`);\n  if (fs.existsSync(cacheFile)) {\n    const c = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(cacheFile, 'utf8'));\n    if (c.length > 100) { console.log(`  [cache] ${symbol}: ${c.length} candles`); return c; }\n  }\n  const endTime = Date.now();\n  const startTime = endTime - DAYS * 24 * 3600e3;\n  let cursor = startTime, all = [], host = null;\n  // descobre host alcan\u00e7\u00e1vel\n  for (const h of HOSTS) {\n    const r = await httpsGetJSON(h, `/api/v3/klines?symbol=${symbol}&interval=${INTERVAL}&limit=1`).catch(() => null);\n    if (r && r.status === 200 && Array.isArray(r.body)) { host = h; break; }\n  }\n  if (!host) throw new Error(`nenhum host Binance alcan\u00e7\u00e1vel para ${symbol} (tentei ${HOSTS.join(', ')})`);\n  while (cursor < endTime) {\n    const url = `/api/v3/klines?symbol=${symbol}&interval=${INTERVAL}&startTime=${cursor}&limit=1000`;\n    const r = await httpsGetJSON(host, url);\n    if (r.status !== 200 || !Array.isArray(r.body) || r.body.length === 0) break;\n    const batch = r.body.map(k => ({ t: k[0], open: +k[1], high: +k[2], low: +k[3], close: +k[4], vol: +k[5] }));\n    all.push(...batch);\n    cursor = batch[batch.length - 1].t + BAR_MS;\n    if (batch.length < 1000) break;\n  }\n  // dedup por timestamp\n  const seen = new Set(); const out = [];\n  for (const c of all) { if (!seen.has(c.t)) { seen.add(c.t); out.push(c); } }\n  out.sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);\n  fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });\n  fs.writeFileSync(cacheFile, JSON.stringify(out));\n  console.log(`  [fetch ${host}] ${symbol}: ${out.length} candles`);\n  return out;\n}\n\n// ---------- indicadores (Wilder) \u2014 retornam arrays alinhados ao \u00edndice (NaN no warmup) ----------\nfunction rsiSeries(close, period = 14) {\n  const out = Array(close.length).fill(NaN);\n  if (close.length <= period) return out;\n  let gain = 0, loss = 0;\n  for (let i = 1; i <= period; i++) { const d = close[i] - close[i - 1]; if (d >= 0) gain += d; else loss -= d; }\n  let ag = gain / period, al = loss / period;\n  out[period] = al === 0 ? 100 : 100 - 100 / (1 + ag / al);\n  for (let i = period + 1; i < close.length; i++) {\n    const d = close[i] - close[i - 1];\n    ag = (ag * (period - 1) + (d > 0 ? d : 0)) / period;\n    al = (al * (period - 1) + (d < 0 ? -d : 0)) / period;\n    out[i] = al === 0 ? 100 : 100 - 100 / (1 + ag / al);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\nfunction trueRange(high, low, close, i) {\n  if (i === 0) return high[i] - low[i];\n  return Math.max(high[i] - low[i], Math.abs(high[i] - close[i - 1]), Math.abs(low[i] - close[i - 1]));\n}\nfunction atrSeries(high, low, close, period = 14) {\n  const out = Array(close.length).fill(NaN);\n  if (close.length <= period) return out;\n  let tr = 0; for (let i = 1; i <= period; i++) tr += trueRange(high, low, close, i);\n  let atr = tr / period; out[period] = atr;\n  for (let i = period + 1; i < close.length; i++) { atr = (atr * (period - 1) + trueRange(high, low, close, i)) / period; out[i] = atr; }\n  return out;\n}\nfunction adxSeries(high, low, close, period = 14) {\n  const n = close.length, out = Array(n).fill(NaN);\n  if (n <= 2 * period) return out;\n  const plusDM = Array(n).fill(0), minusDM = Array(n).fill(0), tr = Array(n).fill(0);\n  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {\n    const up = high[i] - high[i - 1], dn = low[i - 1] - low[i];\n    plusDM[i] = (up > dn && up > 0) ? up : 0;\n    minusDM[i] = (dn > up && dn > 0) ? dn : 0;\n    tr[i] = trueRange(high, low, close, i);\n  }\n  // Wilder smoothing\n  let sTR = 0, sP = 0, sM = 0;\n  for (let i = 1; i <= period; i++) { sTR += tr[i]; sP += plusDM[i]; sM += minusDM[i]; }\n  const dx = Array(n).fill(NaN);\n  function dxAt(sP, sM, sTR) { const pdi = 100 * sP / sTR, mdi = 100 * sM / sTR; const den = pdi + mdi; return den === 0 ? 0 : 100 * Math.abs(pdi - mdi) / den; }\n  dx[period] = dxAt(sP, sM, sTR);\n  for (let i = period + 1; i < n; i++) {\n    sTR = sTR - sTR / period + tr[i];\n    sP = sP - sP / period + plusDM[i];\n    sM = sM - sM / period + minusDM[i];\n    dx[i] = dxAt(sP, sM, sTR);\n  }\n  // ADX = m\u00e9dia Wilder do DX, come\u00e7ando ap\u00f3s 2*period\n  let adx = 0; for (let i = period + 1; i <= 2 * period; i++) adx += dx[i]; adx /= period;\n  out[2 * period] = adx;\n  for (let i = 2 * period + 1; i < n; i++) { adx = (adx * (period - 1) + dx[i]) / period; out[i] = adx; }\n  return out;\n}\nfunction smaSeries(close, period) {\n  const out = Array(close.length).fill(NaN); let s = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < close.length; i++) { s += close[i]; if (i >= period) s -= close[i - period]; if (i >= period - 1) out[i] = s / period; }\n  return out;\n}\nfunction realizedVolSeries(close, window = 24) {\n  const out = Array(close.length).fill(NaN);\n  const lr = close.map((c, i) => i ? Math.log(c / close[i - 1]) : 0);\n  for (let i = window; i < close.length; i++) out[i] = std(lr.slice(i - window + 1, i + 1)) * Math.sqrt(BARS_PER_YEAR);\n  return out;\n}\n\n// ---------- engine de backtest (long-only spot, exec no OPEN do candle seguinte = sem lookahead) ----------\nfunction backtest(candles, signalFn, startIdx, endIdx) {\n  let inPos = false, entry = 0, equity = 1, peak = 1, maxDD = 0;\n  const trades = [], rets = [];\n  for (let i = startIdx; i < endIdx; i++) {\n    const sig = signalFn(i, inPos);\n    const nextOpen = (i + 1 < candles.length) ? candles[i + 1].open : null;\n    if (sig === 'BUY' && !inPos && nextOpen) { entry = nextOpen * (1 + FEE); inPos = true; }\n    else if (sig === 'SELL' && inPos && nextOpen) {\n      const exit = nextOpen * (1 - FEE); const r = exit / entry - 1;\n      trades.push(r); equity *= (1 + r); inPos = false;\n    }\n    // equity mark-to-market p/ drawdown e s\u00e9rie de retornos por candle\n    const mtm = inPos ? equity * (candles[i].close / entry) : equity;\n    if (mtm > peak) peak = mtm; const dd = mtm / peak - 1; if (dd < maxDD) maxDD = dd;\n    if (i > startIdx) {\n      const prevMtm = rets._prev !== undefined ? rets._prev : mtm;\n      rets.push(prevMtm === 0 ? 0 : mtm / prevMtm - 1);\n    }\n    rets._prev = mtm;\n  }\n  // fecha posi\u00e7\u00e3o aberta no fim (no close do \u00faltimo candle)\n  if (inPos) { const exit = candles[endIdx - 1].close * (1 - FEE); const r = exit / entry - 1; trades.push(r); equity *= (1 + r); }\n  const sharpe = std(rets) === 0 ? 0 : (mean(rets) / std(rets)) * Math.sqrt(BARS_PER_YEAR);\n  return {\n    netReturn: equity - 1, nTrades: trades.length,\n    winRate: trades.length ? trades.filter(r => r > 0).length / trades.length : 0,\n    avgTrade: trades.length ? mean(trades) : 0,\n    maxDD, sharpe,\n  };\n}\nfunction buyHold(candles, startIdx, endIdx) {\n  const e = candles[startIdx].open * (1 + FEE), x = candles[endIdx - 1].close * (1 - FEE);\n  return { netReturn: x / e - 1, nTrades: 1 };\n}\n\n// ---------- estrat\u00e9gias ----------\nconst meanReversion = (ind, lo = 30, hi = 70) => (i, inPos) => {\n  const r = ind.rsi[i]; if (!Number.isFinite(r)) return null;\n  if (!inPos && r < lo) return 'BUY';\n  if (inPos && r > hi) return 'SELL';\n  return null;\n};\nconst trendFollow = (ind, fast = 20, slow = 50) => (i, inPos) => {\n  const f = ind.smaFast[i], s = ind.smaSlow[i], fp = ind.smaFast[i - 1], sp = ind.smaSlow[i - 1];\n  if (![f, s, fp, sp].every(Number.isFinite)) return null;\n  if (!inPos && fp <= sp && f > s) return 'BUY';     // cruzou p/ cima\n  if (inPos && fp >= sp && f < s) return 'SELL';      // cruzou p/ baixo\n  return null;\n};\n\n// ---------- regime (sem lookahead: usa indicadores at\u00e9 i) ----------\nfunction regimeAt(ind, i) {\n  const adx = ind.adx[i];\n  if (!Number.isFinite(adx)) return 'mixed';\n  if (adx >= 25) return 'trend';\n  if (adx < 20) return 'range';\n  return 'mixed';\n}\n\n// ---------- walk-forward: janelas OOS; regime no in\u00edcio da janela escolhe a estrat\u00e9gia ----------\nfunction walkForward(candles, ind, opts) {\n  const test = opts.testBars, warm = opts.warmBars;\n  const fixedMR = [], fixedTR = [], regime = [], bh = [];\n  const regimeCounts = { trend: 0, range: 0, mixed: 0 };\n  let start = warm;\n  const mrFn = meanReversion(ind, opts.lo, opts.hi), trFn = trendFollow(ind, opts.fast, opts.slow);\n  while (start + test <= candles.length) {\n    const a = start, b = start + test;\n    // classifica regime no \u00daLTIMO candle antes da janela (a-1) \u2014 info dispon\u00edvel ex-ante\n    const reg = regimeAt(ind, a - 1);\n    regimeCounts[reg]++;\n    const mr = backtest(candles, mrFn, a, b).netReturn;\n    const tr = backtest(candles, trFn, a, b).netReturn;\n    const chosen = reg === 'trend' ? tr : reg === 'range' ? mr : (mr + tr) / 2; // mixed = neutro/meio\n    fixedMR.push(mr); fixedTR.push(tr); regime.push(chosen); bh.push(buyHold(candles, a, b).netReturn);\n    start += test;\n  }\n  const agg = arr => ({ total: arr.reduce((s, r) => s * (1 + r), 1) - 1, avg: mean(arr), winRate: arr.filter(r => r > 0).length / (arr.length || 1), nWin: arr.length });\n  return { windows: fixedMR.length, regimeCounts, fixedMR: agg(fixedMR), fixedTR: agg(fixedTR), regimeSwitch: agg(regime), buyHold: agg(bh) };\n}\n\n// ---------- main ----------\n(async () => {\n  fs.mkdirSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true });\n  console.log(`TradeUX backtest \u2014 ${INTERVAL}, ${DAYS}d, fee ${FEE * 100}%/lado, s\u00edmbolos: ${SYMBOLS.join(', ')}`);\n  const report = { generatedDays: DAYS, interval: INTERVAL, fee: FEE, symbols: {}, params: {} };\n  const lines = [];\n  lines.push(`# TradeUX \u2014 Backtest robusto + regime (${INTERVAL}, ${DAYS}d, fee ${FEE * 100}%/lado)\\n`);\n  lines.push(`> Long-only spot. Execu\u00e7\u00e3o no **open do candle seguinte** ao sinal (sem lookahead). Net de fee.\\n`);\n\n  for (const sym of SYMBOLS) {\n    let candles;\n    try { candles = await fetchAllKlines(sym); } catch (e) { console.log(`  ER\u0420\u041e ${sym}: ${e.message}`); lines.push(`\\n## ${sym}\\n\\n_falha ao obter dados: ${e.message}_\\n`); continue; }\n    if (!candles || candles.length < 300) { lines.push(`\\n## ${sym}\\n\\n_dados insuficientes (${candles ? candles.length : 0} candles)_\\n`); continue; }\n    const close = candles.map(c => c.close), high = candles.map(c => c.high), low = candles.map(c => c.low);\n    const ind = {\n      rsi: rsiSeries(close, 14), adx: adxSeries(high, low, close, 14), atr: atrSeries(high, low, close, 14),\n      smaFast: smaSeries(close, 20), smaSlow: smaSeries(close, 50), vol: realizedVolSeries(close, 24),\n    };\n    const warm = 60; // warmup p/ indicadores estabilizarem\n    const periodDays = ((candles[candles.length - 1].t - candles[0].t) / 864e5).toFixed(0);\n    // estrat\u00e9gias fixas no per\u00edodo inteiro\n    const mrFull = backtest(candles, meanReversion(ind, 30, 70), warm, candles.length);\n    const trFull = backtest(candles, trendFollow(ind, 20, 50), warm, candles.length);\n    const bhFull = buyHold(candles, warm, candles.length);\n    // walk-forward OOS (janela de teste = 30 dias)\n    const testBars = Math.round(30 * 864e5 / BAR_MS);\n    const wf = walkForward(candles, ind, { testBars, warmBars: warm, lo: 30, hi: 70, fast: 20, slow: 50 });\n    // perfil de regime no per\u00edodo\n    const regCount = { trend: 0, range: 0, mixed: 0 };\n    for (let i = warm; i < candles.length; i++) regCount[regimeAt(ind, i)]++;\n    const totReg = regCount.trend + regCount.range + regCount.mixed;\n\n    report.symbols[sym] = { candles: candles.length, periodDays: +periodDays, full: { mr: mrFull, tr: trFull, bh: bhFull }, walkForward: wf, regimeProfile: regCount };\n    console.log(`  ${sym}: MR ${pct(mrFull.netReturn)} (${mrFull.nTrades}t) | TR ${pct(trFull.netReturn)} (${trFull.nTrades}t) | B&H ${pct(bhFull.netReturn)} | WF regime ${pct(wf.regimeSwitch.total)}`);\n\n    lines.push(`\\n## ${sym} \u2014 ${candles.length} candles (${periodDays}d)\\n`);\n    lines.push(`**Perfil de regime** (ADX): trend ${pct(regCount.trend / totReg)} \u00b7 range ${pct(regCount.range / totReg)} \u00b7 mixed ${pct(regCount.mixed / totReg)}\\n`);\n    lines.push(`### Per\u00edodo inteiro (in-sample)\\n`);\n    lines.push(`| Estrat\u00e9gia | Retorno net | Trades | Win% | Avg/trade | MaxDD | Sharpe |`);\n    lines.push(`|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|`);\n    lines.push(`| Mean-reversion RSI 30/70 | ${pct(mrFull.netReturn)} | ${mrFull.nTrades} | ${pct(mrFull.winRate)} | ${pct(mrFull.avgTrade)} | ${pct(mrFull.maxDD)} | ${round(mrFull.sharpe, 2)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Trend SMA 20/50 | ${pct(trFull.netReturn)} | ${trFull.nTrades} | ${pct(trFull.winRate)} | ${pct(trFull.avgTrade)} | ${pct(trFull.maxDD)} | ${round(trFull.sharpe, 2)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Buy & Hold | ${pct(bhFull.netReturn)} | 1 | \u2014 | \u2014 | \u2014 | \u2014 |`);\n    lines.push(`\\n### Walk-forward OUT-OF-SAMPLE (${wf.windows} janelas de 30d; regime ex-ante escolhe a estrat\u00e9gia)\\n`);\n    lines.push(`| Abordagem | Retorno composto OOS | Avg/janela | Janelas positivas |`);\n    lines.push(`|---|---|---|---|`);\n    lines.push(`| **Regime-switching** | **${pct(wf.regimeSwitch.total)}** | ${pct(wf.regimeSwitch.avg)} | ${pct(wf.regimeSwitch.winRate)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Sempre mean-reversion | ${pct(wf.fixedMR.total)} | ${pct(wf.fixedMR.avg)} | ${pct(wf.fixedMR.winRate)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Sempre trend | ${pct(wf.fixedTR.total)} | ${pct(wf.fixedTR.avg)} | ${pct(wf.fixedTR.winRate)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Buy & Hold | ${pct(wf.buyHold.total)} | ${pct(wf.buyHold.avg)} | ${pct(wf.buyHold.winRate)} |`);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- sensibilidade de par\u00e2metros (agregada nos s\u00edmbolos, per\u00edodo inteiro) ----------\n  lines.push(`\\n## Sensibilidade de par\u00e2metros (retorno net m\u00e9dio entre s\u00edmbolos, per\u00edodo inteiro)\\n`);\n  lines.push(`### Mean-reversion \u2014 thresholds RSI\\n`);\n  lines.push(`| lo/hi | retorno m\u00e9dio | trades m\u00e9dios |`); lines.push(`|---|---|---|`);\n  const dataBySym = {};\n  for (const sym of Object.keys(report.symbols)) {\n    const cf = path.join(DATA_DIR, `${sym}-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.json`);\n    if (fs.existsSync(cf)) dataBySym[sym] = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(cf, 'utf8'));\n  }\n  for (const [lo, hi] of [[25, 75], [30, 70], [35, 65], [20, 80]]) {\n    const rs = [], ts = [];\n    for (const sym of Object.keys(dataBySym)) {\n      const cd = dataBySym[sym]; const cl = cd.map(c => c.close);\n      const ind = { rsi: rsiSeries(cl, 14) };\n      const r = backtest(cd, meanReversion(ind, lo, hi), 60, cd.length);\n      rs.push(r.netReturn); ts.push(r.nTrades);\n    }\n    report.params[`rsi_${lo}_${hi}`] = { avgReturn: mean(rs), avgTrades: mean(ts) };\n    lines.push(`| ${lo}/${hi} | ${pct(mean(rs))} | ${mean(ts).toFixed(1)} |`);\n  }\n  lines.push(`\\n### Trend \u2014 janelas SMA (fast/slow)\\n`);\n  lines.push(`| fast/slow | retorno m\u00e9dio | trades m\u00e9dios |`); lines.push(`|---|---|---|`);\n  for (const [fast, slow] of [[10, 30], [20, 50], [50, 200]]) {\n    const rs = [], ts = [];\n    for (const sym of Object.keys(dataBySym)) {\n      const cd = dataBySym[sym]; const cl = cd.map(c => c.close);\n      const ind = { smaFast: smaSeries(cl, fast), smaSlow: smaSeries(cl, slow) };\n      const r = backtest(cd, trendFollow(ind, fast, slow), Math.max(60, slow + 5), cd.length);\n      rs.push(r.netReturn); ts.push(r.nTrades);\n    }\n    report.params[`sma_${fast}_${slow}`] = { avgReturn: mean(rs), avgTrades: mean(ts) };\n    lines.push(`| ${fast}/${slow} | ${pct(mean(rs))} | ${mean(ts).toFixed(1)} |`);\n  }\n\n  lines.push(`\\n---\\n_Gerado pelo harness de backtest do TradeUX. Dados: Binance klines reais (cache em backtest-data/). N\u00e3o \u00e9 conselho financeiro; testnet/valida\u00e7\u00e3o._`);\n  const md = lines.join('\\n');\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT_DIR, `backtest-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.md`), md);\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT_DIR, `backtest-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.json`), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));\n  console.log(`\\nRelat\u00f3rio: backend/backtest-results/backtest-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.md`);\n})();\n"
}
```

Antes do run completo (4 símbolos, 1 ano), faço 3 refinamentos de robustez que notei revisando: (1) classificar regime pela **ADX média da janela anterior** (não 1 ponto ruidoso); (2) regime "mixed" → default mean-reversion (baseline real, não um blend impossível); (3) adicionar a **tabela agregada OOS entre símbolos** — o resultado científico de verdade (edge vs sorte):

```json
}
```

## 🤖 Claude *(16:27:24)*

Resultado completo do ano: **tudo perdeu** (B&H BTC -36%, ETH -26%, SOL -51% — foi um ano de baixa). Isso é uma descoberta honesta e importante: **estratégias long-only não escapam de bear market**, e o regime-switch simples (MR↔trend, ambos comprados) também sangra. Isso *corrige* o otimismo do backtest de 83d da sessão anterior.

A lição científica: falta um **estado "caixa"** (ficar fora em tendência de baixa). Vou adicionar isso — um regime de 3 estados (bear→caixa / trend→trend / range→mean-reversion) usando SMA200, mais um baseline long/flat. É o ingrediente que realmente pode bater buy&hold num ano ruim. Reescrevendo o script com essa melhoria:

## 🤖 Claude *(16:28:49)*


🛠️ **Tool Call**: `Write`
```json
{
  "file_path": "/tmp/backtest.js",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env node\n/*\n * TradeUX \u2014 Backtest robusto + an\u00e1lise de regime (walk-forward, fee-adjusted).\n * Self-contained: s\u00f3 usa built-ins do Node. Indicadores (RSI/ATR/ADX/vol) em Wilder.\n *\n * Objetivo (valida\u00e7\u00e3o rumo aos 500k, testnet): separar EDGE de SORTE.\n *  - dados reais 1h da Binance (mainnet p\u00fablica), ~1 ano, m\u00faltiplos s\u00edmbolos\n *  - estrat\u00e9gias long-only spot: mean-reversion (RSI) vs trend (SMA cross) vs long/flat (SMA200)\n *  - walk-forward OUT-OF-SAMPLE + regime-switching 3-estados (bear\u2192CAIXA / trend / range)\n *  - sensibilidade de par\u00e2metros (RSI thresholds, SMA windows)\n *  - m\u00e9tricas net-de-fee: retorno, n\u00ba trades, win%, avg/trade, maxDD, Sharpe, vs buy&hold\n *\n * Uso: node scripts/backtest.js [--days=365] [--interval=1h] [--symbols=BTCUSDT,ETHUSDT,...]\n */\n'use strict';\nconst https = require('https');\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst path = require('path');\n\n// ---------- config ----------\nconst args = Object.fromEntries(process.argv.slice(2).map(a => {\n  const m = a.match(/^--([^=]+)=?(.*)$/); return m ? [m[1], m[2] || true] : [a, true];\n}));\nconst FEE = 0.001;                       // 0.1% por lado (taker)\nconst INTERVAL = args.interval || '1h';\nconst DAYS = parseInt(args.days || '365', 10);\nconst SYMBOLS = (args.symbols || 'BTCUSDT,ETHUSDT,BNBUSDT,SOLUSDT').split(',');\nconst HOSTS = ['api.binance.com', 'data-api.binance.vision', 'api.binance.us'];\nconst MS_PER = { '1h': 3600e3, '4h': 4 * 3600e3, '1d': 24 * 3600e3, '15m': 15 * 60e3 };\nconst BAR_MS = MS_PER[INTERVAL] || 3600e3;\nconst BARS_PER_YEAR = (365 * 24 * 3600e3) / BAR_MS;\nconst DATA_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'backtest-data');\nconst OUT_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'backtest-results');\n\n// ---------- util ----------\nconst mean = a => a.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) / (a.length || 1);\nconst std = a => { if (a.length < 2) return 0; const m = mean(a); return Math.sqrt(mean(a.map(x => (x - m) ** 2))); };\nconst pct = x => (x * 100).toFixed(2) + '%';\nconst round = (x, n = 4) => Number.isFinite(x) ? Number(x.toFixed(n)) : null;\n\nfunction httpsGetJSON(host, pathUrl) {\n  return new Promise((res, rej) => {\n    https.get({ host, path: pathUrl, headers: { 'User-Agent': 'tradeux-backtest' } }, r => {\n      let d = ''; r.on('data', c => d += c);\n      r.on('end', () => { try { res({ status: r.statusCode, body: JSON.parse(d) }); } catch (e) { res({ status: r.statusCode, body: d }); } });\n    }).on('error', rej);\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------- data fetch (klines paginados + cache em disco) ----------\nasync function fetchAllKlines(symbol) {\n  const cacheFile = path.join(DATA_DIR, `${symbol}-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.json`);\n  if (fs.existsSync(cacheFile)) {\n    const c = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(cacheFile, 'utf8'));\n    if (c.length > 100) { console.log(`  [cache] ${symbol}: ${c.length} candles`); return c; }\n  }\n  const endTime = Date.now();\n  const startTime = endTime - DAYS * 24 * 3600e3;\n  let cursor = startTime, all = [], host = null;\n  for (const h of HOSTS) {\n    const r = await httpsGetJSON(h, `/api/v3/klines?symbol=${symbol}&interval=${INTERVAL}&limit=1`).catch(() => null);\n    if (r && r.status === 200 && Array.isArray(r.body)) { host = h; break; }\n  }\n  if (!host) throw new Error(`nenhum host Binance alcan\u00e7\u00e1vel para ${symbol} (tentei ${HOSTS.join(', ')})`);\n  while (cursor < endTime) {\n    const url = `/api/v3/klines?symbol=${symbol}&interval=${INTERVAL}&startTime=${cursor}&limit=1000`;\n    const r = await httpsGetJSON(host, url);\n    if (r.status !== 200 || !Array.isArray(r.body) || r.body.length === 0) break;\n    const batch = r.body.map(k => ({ t: k[0], open: +k[1], high: +k[2], low: +k[3], close: +k[4], vol: +k[5] }));\n    all.push(...batch);\n    cursor = batch[batch.length - 1].t + BAR_MS;\n    if (batch.length < 1000) break;\n  }\n  const seen = new Set(); const out = [];\n  for (const c of all) { if (!seen.has(c.t)) { seen.add(c.t); out.push(c); } }\n  out.sort((a, b) => a.t - b.t);\n  fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });\n  fs.writeFileSync(cacheFile, JSON.stringify(out));\n  console.log(`  [fetch ${host}] ${symbol}: ${out.length} candles`);\n  return out;\n}\n\n// ---------- indicadores (Wilder) \u2014 arrays alinhados ao \u00edndice (NaN no warmup) ----------\nfunction rsiSeries(close, period = 14) {\n  const out = Array(close.length).fill(NaN);\n  if (close.length <= period) return out;\n  let gain = 0, loss = 0;\n  for (let i = 1; i <= period; i++) { const d = close[i] - close[i - 1]; if (d >= 0) gain += d; else loss -= d; }\n  let ag = gain / period, al = loss / period;\n  out[period] = al === 0 ? 100 : 100 - 100 / (1 + ag / al);\n  for (let i = period + 1; i < close.length; i++) {\n    const d = close[i] - close[i - 1];\n    ag = (ag * (period - 1) + (d > 0 ? d : 0)) / period;\n    al = (al * (period - 1) + (d < 0 ? -d : 0)) / period;\n    out[i] = al === 0 ? 100 : 100 - 100 / (1 + ag / al);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\nfunction trueRange(high, low, close, i) {\n  if (i === 0) return high[i] - low[i];\n  return Math.max(high[i] - low[i], Math.abs(high[i] - close[i - 1]), Math.abs(low[i] - close[i - 1]));\n}\nfunction atrSeries(high, low, close, period = 14) {\n  const out = Array(close.length).fill(NaN);\n  if (close.length <= period) return out;\n  let tr = 0; for (let i = 1; i <= period; i++) tr += trueRange(high, low, close, i);\n  let atr = tr / period; out[period] = atr;\n  for (let i = period + 1; i < close.length; i++) { atr = (atr * (period - 1) + trueRange(high, low, close, i)) / period; out[i] = atr; }\n  return out;\n}\nfunction adxSeries(high, low, close, period = 14) {\n  const n = close.length, out = Array(n).fill(NaN);\n  if (n <= 2 * period) return out;\n  const plusDM = Array(n).fill(0), minusDM = Array(n).fill(0), tr = Array(n).fill(0);\n  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {\n    const up = high[i] - high[i - 1], dn = low[i - 1] - low[i];\n    plusDM[i] = (up > dn && up > 0) ? up : 0;\n    minusDM[i] = (dn > up && dn > 0) ? dn : 0;\n    tr[i] = trueRange(high, low, close, i);\n  }\n  let sTR = 0, sP = 0, sM = 0;\n  for (let i = 1; i <= period; i++) { sTR += tr[i]; sP += plusDM[i]; sM += minusDM[i]; }\n  const dx = Array(n).fill(NaN);\n  const dxAt = (sP, sM, sTR) => { const pdi = 100 * sP / sTR, mdi = 100 * sM / sTR; const den = pdi + mdi; return den === 0 ? 0 : 100 * Math.abs(pdi - mdi) / den; };\n  dx[period] = dxAt(sP, sM, sTR);\n  for (let i = period + 1; i < n; i++) {\n    sTR = sTR - sTR / period + tr[i]; sP = sP - sP / period + plusDM[i]; sM = sM - sM / period + minusDM[i];\n    dx[i] = dxAt(sP, sM, sTR);\n  }\n  let adx = 0; for (let i = period + 1; i <= 2 * period; i++) adx += dx[i]; adx /= period;\n  out[2 * period] = adx;\n  for (let i = 2 * period + 1; i < n; i++) { adx = (adx * (period - 1) + dx[i]) / period; out[i] = adx; }\n  return out;\n}\nfunction smaSeries(close, period) {\n  const out = Array(close.length).fill(NaN); let s = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < close.length; i++) { s += close[i]; if (i >= period) s -= close[i - period]; if (i >= period - 1) out[i] = s / period; }\n  return out;\n}\nfunction realizedVolSeries(close, window = 24) {\n  const out = Array(close.length).fill(NaN);\n  const lr = close.map((c, i) => i ? Math.log(c / close[i - 1]) : 0);\n  for (let i = window; i < close.length; i++) out[i] = std(lr.slice(i - window + 1, i + 1)) * Math.sqrt(BARS_PER_YEAR);\n  return out;\n}\n\n// ---------- engine (long-only spot, exec no OPEN do candle seguinte = sem lookahead) ----------\nfunction backtest(candles, signalFn, startIdx, endIdx) {\n  let inPos = false, entry = 0, equity = 1, peak = 1, maxDD = 0, prevMtm;\n  const trades = [], rets = [];\n  for (let i = startIdx; i < endIdx; i++) {\n    const sig = signalFn(i, inPos);\n    const nextOpen = (i + 1 < candles.length) ? candles[i + 1].open : null;\n    if (sig === 'BUY' && !inPos && nextOpen) { entry = nextOpen * (1 + FEE); inPos = true; }\n    else if (sig === 'SELL' && inPos && nextOpen) { const exit = nextOpen * (1 - FEE); const r = exit / entry - 1; trades.push(r); equity *= (1 + r); inPos = false; }\n    const mtm = inPos ? equity * (candles[i].close / entry) : equity;\n    if (mtm > peak) peak = mtm; const dd = mtm / peak - 1; if (dd < maxDD) maxDD = dd;\n    if (prevMtm !== undefined) rets.push(prevMtm === 0 ? 0 : mtm / prevMtm - 1);\n    prevMtm = mtm;\n  }\n  if (inPos) { const exit = candles[endIdx - 1].close * (1 - FEE); const r = exit / entry - 1; trades.push(r); equity *= (1 + r); }\n  const sharpe = std(rets) === 0 ? 0 : (mean(rets) / std(rets)) * Math.sqrt(BARS_PER_YEAR);\n  return {\n    netReturn: equity - 1, nTrades: trades.length,\n    winRate: trades.length ? trades.filter(r => r > 0).length / trades.length : 0,\n    avgTrade: trades.length ? mean(trades) : 0, maxDD, sharpe,\n  };\n}\nfunction buyHold(candles, startIdx, endIdx) {\n  const e = candles[startIdx].open * (1 + FEE), x = candles[endIdx - 1].close * (1 - FEE);\n  return { netReturn: x / e - 1, nTrades: 1 };\n}\n\n// ---------- estrat\u00e9gias ----------\nconst meanReversion = (ind, lo = 30, hi = 70) => (i, inPos) => {\n  const r = ind.rsi[i]; if (!Number.isFinite(r)) return null;\n  if (!inPos && r < lo) return 'BUY';\n  if (inPos && r > hi) return 'SELL';\n  return null;\n};\nconst trendFollow = (ind, fast = 20, slow = 50) => (i, inPos) => {\n  const f = ind.smaFast[i], s = ind.smaSlow[i], fp = ind.smaFast[i - 1], sp = ind.smaSlow[i - 1];\n  if (![f, s, fp, sp].every(Number.isFinite)) return null;\n  if (!inPos && fp <= sp && f > s) return 'BUY';\n  if (inPos && fp >= sp && f < s) return 'SELL';\n  return null;\n};\n// long/flat: comprado s\u00f3 acima da SMA200 (filtro macro de tend\u00eancia; sen\u00e3o fica em CAIXA)\nconst longFlat = (ind) => (i, inPos) => {\n  const c = ind.close[i], s = ind.sma200[i];\n  if (![c, s].every(Number.isFinite)) return null;\n  if (!inPos && c > s) return 'BUY';\n  if (inPos && c < s) return 'SELL';\n  return null;\n};\n\n// ---------- regime (sem lookahead) ----------\n// 3-estados na janela [from,to): bear (maioria abaixo da SMA200) / trend (ADX m\u00e9dio\u226525) / range (<20) / mixed\nfunction regimeWindow3(ind, from, to) {\n  let below = 0, n = 0; const adxs = [];\n  for (let i = Math.max(0, from); i < to; i++) {\n    if (Number.isFinite(ind.sma200[i])) { n++; if (ind.close[i] < ind.sma200[i]) below++; }\n    if (Number.isFinite(ind.adx[i])) adxs.push(ind.adx[i]);\n  }\n  if (n && below / n > 0.6) return 'bear';\n  const m = adxs.length ? mean(adxs) : 0;\n  if (m >= 25) return 'trend';\n  if (m < 20) return 'range';\n  return 'mixed';\n}\n\n// ---------- walk-forward: janelas OOS; regime ex-ante (janela anterior) escolhe a estrat\u00e9gia ----------\nfunction walkForward(candles, ind, opts) {\n  const test = opts.testBars, warm = opts.warmBars;\n  const S = { mr: [], tr: [], lf: [], regime: [], bh: [] };\n  const regimeCounts = { trend: 0, range: 0, bear: 0, mixed: 0 };\n  const mrFn = meanReversion(ind, opts.lo, opts.hi), trFn = trendFollow(ind, opts.fast, opts.slow), lfFn = longFlat(ind);\n  let start = warm;\n  while (start + test <= candles.length) {\n    const a = start, b = start + test;\n    const reg = regimeWindow3(ind, a - test, a);  // regime da janela ANTERIOR (ex-ante)\n    regimeCounts[reg]++;\n    const mr = backtest(candles, mrFn, a, b).netReturn;\n    const tr = backtest(candles, trFn, a, b).netReturn;\n    const lf = backtest(candles, lfFn, a, b).netReturn;\n    // regime-switch 3-estados: bear->CAIXA(0); trend->trend; range/mixed->mean-reversion\n    const chosen = reg === 'bear' ? 0 : reg === 'trend' ? tr : mr;\n    S.mr.push(mr); S.tr.push(tr); S.lf.push(lf); S.regime.push(chosen); S.bh.push(buyHold(candles, a, b).netReturn);\n    start += test;\n  }\n  const agg = arr => ({ total: arr.reduce((s, r) => s * (1 + r), 1) - 1, avg: mean(arr), winRate: arr.filter(r => r > 0).length / (arr.length || 1) });\n  return { windows: S.bh.length, regimeCounts, fixedMR: agg(S.mr), fixedTR: agg(S.tr), longFlat: agg(S.lf), regimeSwitch: agg(S.regime), buyHold: agg(S.bh) };\n}\n\n// ---------- main ----------\n(async () => {\n  fs.mkdirSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true });\n  console.log(`TradeUX backtest \u2014 ${INTERVAL}, ${DAYS}d, fee ${FEE * 100}%/lado, s\u00edmbolos: ${SYMBOLS.join(', ')}`);\n  const report = { generatedDays: DAYS, interval: INTERVAL, fee: FEE, symbols: {}, params: {} };\n  const lines = [];\n  lines.push(`# TradeUX \u2014 Backtest robusto + regime (${INTERVAL}, ${DAYS}d, fee ${FEE * 100}%/lado)\\n`);\n  lines.push(`> Long-only spot. Execu\u00e7\u00e3o no **open do candle seguinte** ao sinal (sem lookahead). Tudo net de fee.`);\n  lines.push(`> Regime-switching 3-estados (ex-ante, ADX+SMA200): **bear\u2192caixa**, **trend\u2192trend (SMA 20/50)**, **range\u2192mean-reversion (RSI 30/70)**.\\n`);\n\n  for (const sym of SYMBOLS) {\n    let candles;\n    try { candles = await fetchAllKlines(sym); } catch (e) { console.log(`  ERRO ${sym}: ${e.message}`); lines.push(`\\n## ${sym}\\n\\n_falha ao obter dados: ${e.message}_\\n`); continue; }\n    if (!candles || candles.length < 300) { lines.push(`\\n## ${sym}\\n\\n_dados insuficientes_\\n`); continue; }\n    const close = candles.map(c => c.close), high = candles.map(c => c.high), low = candles.map(c => c.low);\n    const ind = {\n      close, rsi: rsiSeries(close, 14), adx: adxSeries(high, low, close, 14), atr: atrSeries(high, low, close, 14),\n      smaFast: smaSeries(close, 20), smaSlow: smaSeries(close, 50), sma200: smaSeries(close, 200), vol: realizedVolSeries(close, 24),\n    };\n    const warm = 210; // warmup p/ SMA200 + indicadores estabilizarem\n    const periodDays = ((candles[candles.length - 1].t - candles[0].t) / 864e5).toFixed(0);\n    const mrFull = backtest(candles, meanReversion(ind, 30, 70), warm, candles.length);\n    const trFull = backtest(candles, trendFollow(ind, 20, 50), warm, candles.length);\n    const lfFull = backtest(candles, longFlat(ind), warm, candles.length);\n    const bhFull = buyHold(candles, warm, candles.length);\n    const testBars = Math.round(30 * 864e5 / BAR_MS);\n    const wf = walkForward(candles, ind, { testBars, warmBars: warm, lo: 30, hi: 70, fast: 20, slow: 50 });\n\n    report.symbols[sym] = { candles: candles.length, periodDays: +periodDays, full: { mr: mrFull, tr: trFull, lf: lfFull, bh: bhFull }, walkForward: wf };\n    console.log(`  ${sym}: MR ${pct(mrFull.netReturn)} | TR ${pct(trFull.netReturn)} | L/F ${pct(lfFull.netReturn)} | B&H ${pct(bhFull.netReturn)} | WF-regime ${pct(wf.regimeSwitch.total)} (bear ${wf.regimeCounts.bear}/${wf.windows} jan)`);\n\n    lines.push(`\\n## ${sym} \u2014 ${candles.length} candles (${periodDays}d)\\n`);\n    lines.push(`### Per\u00edodo inteiro (in-sample)\\n`);\n    lines.push(`| Estrat\u00e9gia | Retorno net | Trades | Win% | Avg/trade | MaxDD | Sharpe |`);\n    lines.push(`|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|`);\n    lines.push(`| Mean-reversion RSI 30/70 | ${pct(mrFull.netReturn)} | ${mrFull.nTrades} | ${pct(mrFull.winRate)} | ${pct(mrFull.avgTrade)} | ${pct(mrFull.maxDD)} | ${round(mrFull.sharpe, 2)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Trend SMA 20/50 | ${pct(trFull.netReturn)} | ${trFull.nTrades} | ${pct(trFull.winRate)} | ${pct(trFull.avgTrade)} | ${pct(trFull.maxDD)} | ${round(trFull.sharpe, 2)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Long/flat SMA200 (caixa em baixa) | ${pct(lfFull.netReturn)} | ${lfFull.nTrades} | ${pct(lfFull.winRate)} | ${pct(lfFull.avgTrade)} | ${pct(lfFull.maxDD)} | ${round(lfFull.sharpe, 2)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Buy & Hold | ${pct(bhFull.netReturn)} | 1 | \u2014 | \u2014 | \u2014 | \u2014 |`);\n    lines.push(`\\n### Walk-forward OUT-OF-SAMPLE (${wf.windows} janelas de 30d) \u2014 regimes: bear ${wf.regimeCounts.bear} \u00b7 trend ${wf.regimeCounts.trend} \u00b7 range ${wf.regimeCounts.range} \u00b7 mixed ${wf.regimeCounts.mixed}\\n`);\n    lines.push(`| Abordagem | Retorno composto OOS | Avg/janela | Janelas positivas |`);\n    lines.push(`|---|---|---|---|`);\n    lines.push(`| **Regime-switching (bear\u2192caixa)** | **${pct(wf.regimeSwitch.total)}** | ${pct(wf.regimeSwitch.avg)} | ${pct(wf.regimeSwitch.winRate)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Long/flat SMA200 | ${pct(wf.longFlat.total)} | ${pct(wf.longFlat.avg)} | ${pct(wf.longFlat.winRate)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Sempre mean-reversion | ${pct(wf.fixedMR.total)} | ${pct(wf.fixedMR.avg)} | ${pct(wf.fixedMR.winRate)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Sempre trend | ${pct(wf.fixedTR.total)} | ${pct(wf.fixedTR.avg)} | ${pct(wf.fixedTR.winRate)} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Buy & Hold | ${pct(wf.buyHold.total)} | ${pct(wf.buyHold.avg)} | ${pct(wf.buyHold.winRate)} |`);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- agregado OOS entre s\u00edmbolos (o resultado cient\u00edfico: edge vs sorte) ----------\n  const aggSyms = Object.keys(report.symbols).filter(s => report.symbols[s].walkForward);\n  if (aggSyms.length) {\n    const totals = key => aggSyms.map(s => report.symbols[s].walkForward[key].total);\n    const beats = (a, b) => aggSyms.filter(s => report.symbols[s].walkForward[a].total > report.symbols[s].walkForward[b].total).length;\n    report.aggregateOOS = {\n      regimeSwitch: mean(totals('regimeSwitch')), longFlat: mean(totals('longFlat')), fixedMR: mean(totals('fixedMR')),\n      fixedTR: mean(totals('fixedTR')), buyHold: mean(totals('buyHold')),\n      regimeBeatsBH: beats('regimeSwitch', 'buyHold'), regimeBeatsMR: beats('regimeSwitch', 'fixedMR'), nSymbols: aggSyms.length,\n    };\n    lines.push(`\\n## \ud83c\udfaf Resultado agregado OUT-OF-SAMPLE (m\u00e9dia entre ${aggSyms.length} s\u00edmbolos)\\n`);\n    lines.push(`| Abordagem | Retorno OOS composto m\u00e9dio | Bate Buy&Hold em |`);\n    lines.push(`|---|---|---|`);\n    lines.push(`| **Regime-switching (bear\u2192caixa)** | **${pct(mean(totals('regimeSwitch')))}** | ${beats('regimeSwitch', 'buyHold')}/${aggSyms.length} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Long/flat SMA200 | ${pct(mean(totals('longFlat')))} | ${beats('longFlat', 'buyHold')}/${aggSyms.length} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Sempre mean-reversion RSI 30/70 | ${pct(mean(totals('fixedMR')))} | ${beats('fixedMR', 'buyHold')}/${aggSyms.length} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Sempre trend SMA 20/50 | ${pct(mean(totals('fixedTR')))} | ${beats('fixedTR', 'buyHold')}/${aggSyms.length} |`);\n    lines.push(`| Buy & Hold | ${pct(mean(totals('buyHold')))} | \u2014 |`);\n    lines.push(`\\n**Leitura:** se o regime-switching (com estado de caixa) bate Buy&Hold em v\u00e1rios s\u00edmbolos OOS, o edge de **trocar de estrat\u00e9gia por regime \u2014 inclusive sair do mercado em baixa \u2014 \u00e9 real**, n\u00e3o sorte de um per\u00edodo. Em ano de baixa, \"ficar em caixa\" \u00e9 o maior edge.\\n`);\n  }\n\n  // ---------- sensibilidade de par\u00e2metros (per\u00edodo inteiro, m\u00e9dia entre s\u00edmbolos) ----------\n  const dataBySym = {};\n  for (const sym of Object.keys(report.symbols)) {\n    const cf = path.join(DATA_DIR, `${sym}-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.json`);\n    if (fs.existsSync(cf)) dataBySym[sym] = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(cf, 'utf8'));\n  }\n  lines.push(`\\n## Sensibilidade de par\u00e2metros (retorno net m\u00e9dio entre s\u00edmbolos, per\u00edodo inteiro)\\n`);\n  lines.push(`### Mean-reversion \u2014 thresholds RSI\\n`);\n  lines.push(`| lo/hi | retorno m\u00e9dio | trades m\u00e9dios |`); lines.push(`|---|---|---|`);\n  for (const [lo, hi] of [[25, 75], [30, 70], [35, 65], [20, 80]]) {\n    const rs = [], ts = [];\n    for (const sym of Object.keys(dataBySym)) {\n      const cd = dataBySym[sym]; const ind = { rsi: rsiSeries(cd.map(c => c.close), 14) };\n      const r = backtest(cd, meanReversion(ind, lo, hi), 210, cd.length); rs.push(r.netReturn); ts.push(r.nTrades);\n    }\n    report.params[`rsi_${lo}_${hi}`] = { avgReturn: mean(rs), avgTrades: mean(ts) };\n    lines.push(`| ${lo}/${hi} | ${pct(mean(rs))} | ${mean(ts).toFixed(1)} |`);\n  }\n  lines.push(`\\n### Trend \u2014 janelas SMA (fast/slow)\\n`);\n  lines.push(`| fast/slow | retorno m\u00e9dio | trades m\u00e9dios |`); lines.push(`|---|---|---|`);\n  for (const [fast, slow] of [[10, 30], [20, 50], [50, 200]]) {\n    const rs = [], ts = [];\n    for (const sym of Object.keys(dataBySym)) {\n      const cd = dataBySym[sym]; const cl = cd.map(c => c.close);\n      const ind = { smaFast: smaSeries(cl, fast), smaSlow: smaSeries(cl, slow) };\n      const r = backtest(cd, trendFollow(ind, fast, slow), Math.max(210, slow + 5), cd.length); rs.push(r.netReturn); ts.push(r.nTrades);\n    }\n    report.params[`sma_${fast}_${slow}`] = { avgReturn: mean(rs), avgTrades: mean(ts) };\n    lines.push(`| ${fast}/${slow} | ${pct(mean(rs))} | ${mean(ts).toFixed(1)} |`);\n  }\n\n  lines.push(`\\n---\\n_Gerado pelo harness de backtest do TradeUX (backend/scripts/backtest.js). Dados: Binance klines reais (cache em backtest-data/, gitignored). Net de fee. N\u00e3o \u00e9 conselho financeiro; fase de valida\u00e7\u00e3o em testnet._`);\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT_DIR, `backtest-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.md`), lines.join('\\n'));\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(OUT_DIR, `backtest-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.json`), JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));\n  console.log(`\\nRelat\u00f3rio: backend/backtest-results/backtest-${INTERVAL}-${DAYS}d.md`);\n})();\n"
}
```

## 🤖 Claude *(16:30:13)*

O backtest melhorado conta a **história científica honesta**:
- **Foi um bear year brutal** (B&H médio -24%). Long-only não escapa — tudo perdeu no agregado.
- **O estado de CAIXA é o maior edge**: no BTC o regime-switch cortou de **-27% (B&H) pra -6.8%** ao detectar "bear" e sair. Mas...
- **O classificador cru (ADX+SMA200) é ruidoso**: bate B&H só em 1/4 símbolos (ajuda no BTC, atrapalha no BNB). O edge existe mas depende da QUALIDADE do classificador.
- **Menos trades perderam menos** (RSI 25/75 ≫ 35/65; SMA 50/200 ≫ 10/30) — fee+whipsaw punem overtrading. Confirma que HFT é matematicamente morto aqui.

Isso é ouro pra validação (corrige o otimismo de amostra pequena). Vou cristalizar essas conclusões no relatório, depois extrair o **detector de regime** como módulo reutilizável. Adicionando a seção de conclusões ao script:

```json
{
  "file_path": "/tmp/regimeClassifier.js",
  "content": "'use strict';\n/*\n * Classificador de regime de mercado do TradeUX \u2014 decide em que \"modo\" operar.\n *\n *   bear  (maioria das velas recentes < SMA200)   -> risk-off: ficar em CAIXA\n *   trend (ADX >= 25)                              -> seguir tend\u00eancia\n *   range (ADX < 20)                               -> mean-reversion (RSI)\n *   mixed (20 <= ADX < 25, n\u00e3o-bear)               -> neutro (default: mean-reversion, baixa convic\u00e7\u00e3o)\n *\n * Mesma l\u00f3gica validada no harness de backtest (backend/scripts/backtest.js). No backtest\n * 1h/1-ano (BTC/ETH/BNB/SOL) o estado de CAIXA foi o maior edge num ano de baixa.\n *\n * Uso como m\u00f3dulo:  const { classifyRegime } = require('./regimeClassifier');\n *                   classifyRegime(candles)  // candles: [{high,low,close}, ...] (antigo->novo)\n * Uso como CLI:     node src/utils/regimeClassifier.js BTCUSDT [1h] [days]\n */\nconst ti = require('technicalindicators');\n\nconst DEFAULTS = { adxPeriod: 14, smaPeriod: 200, rsiPeriod: 14, lookback: 200, bearFrac: 0.6, trendAdx: 25, rangeAdx: 20 };\n\nfunction classifyRegime(candles, opts = {}) {\n  const o = { ...DEFAULTS, ...opts };\n  if (!Array.isArray(candles) || candles.length < o.smaPeriod + 5) {\n    return { regime: 'mixed', strategy: 'mean-reversion', reason: `dados insuficientes (${candles ? candles.length : 0} velas; precisa > ${o.smaPeriod})`, signals: {} };\n  }\n  const high = candles.map(c => +c.high), low = candles.map(c => +c.low), close = candles.map(c => +c.close);\n\n  const adxArr = ti.adx({ high, low, close, period: o.adxPeriod });\n  const adx = adxArr.length ? adxArr[adxArr.length - 1].adx : null;\n  const sma = ti.sma({ values: close, period: o.smaPeriod });\n  const sma200 = sma.length ? sma[sma.length - 1] : null;\n  const rsiArr = ti.rsi({ values: close, period: o.rsiPeriod });\n  const rsi = rsiArr.length ? rsiArr[rsiArr.length - 1] : null;\n  const last = close[close.length - 1];\n\n  // fra\u00e7\u00e3o das \u00faltimas `lookback` velas abaixo da SMA200 (sma alinhado ao fim da s\u00e9rie)\n  const lb = Math.min(o.lookback, sma.length);\n  let below = 0;\n  for (let k = 0; k < lb; k++) {\n    const ci = close.length - 1 - k, si = sma.length - 1 - k;\n    if (si >= 0 && close[ci] < sma[si]) below++;\n  }\n  const belowFrac = lb ? below / lb : 0;\n\n  let regime, strategy, reason;\n  if (belowFrac > o.bearFrac) { regime = 'bear'; strategy = 'cash'; reason = `${(belowFrac * 100).toFixed(0)}% das \u00faltimas ${lb} velas < SMA200 (risk-off)`; }\n  else if (adx != null && adx >= o.trendAdx) { regime = 'trend'; strategy = 'trend-follow'; reason = `ADX ${adx.toFixed(1)} >= ${o.trendAdx} (tend\u00eancia forte)`; }\n  else if (adx != null && adx < o.rangeAdx) { regime = 'range'; strategy = 'mean-reversion'; reason = `ADX ${adx.toFixed(1)} < ${o.rangeAdx} (lateral)`; }\n  else { regime = 'mixed'; strategy = 'mean-reversion'; reason = `ADX ${adx != null ? adx.toFixed(1) : '?'} intermedi\u00e1rio, sem vi\u00e9s claro`; }\n\n  return {\n    regime, strategy, reason,\n    signals: {\n      adx: adx != null ? +adx.toFixed(2) : null,\n      rsi: rsi != null ? +rsi.toFixed(2) : null,\n      lastClose: last,\n      sma200: sma200 != null ? +sma200.toFixed(2) : null,\n      aboveSMA200: sma200 != null ? last > sma200 : null,\n      belowSMA200Frac: +belowFrac.toFixed(3),\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nmodule.exports = { classifyRegime, DEFAULTS };\n\n// ---------- CLI: probe ao vivo (node regimeClassifier.js SYMBOL [interval] [days]) ----------\nif (require.main === module) {\n  const https = require('https');\n  const [symbol = 'BTCUSDT', interval = '1h', days = '20'] = process.argv.slice(2);\n  const hosts = ['api.binance.com', 'data-api.binance.vision', 'api.binance.us'];\n  const get = (host, p) => new Promise((res, rej) => https.get({ host, path: p, headers: { 'User-Agent': 'tradeux' } }, r => { let d = ''; r.on('data', c => d += c); r.on('end', () => { try { res(JSON.parse(d)); } catch (e) { rej(e); } }); }).on('error', rej));\n  (async () => {\n    const start = Date.now() - parseInt(days) * 864e5;\n    for (const h of hosts) {\n      try {\n        const k = await get(h, `/api/v3/klines?symbol=${symbol}&interval=${interval}&startTime=${start}&limit=1000`);\n        if (!Array.isArray(k)) continue;\n        const candles = k.map(c => ({ high: +c[2], low: +c[3], close: +c[4] }));\n        const out = classifyRegime(candles);\n        console.log(JSON.stringify({ symbol, interval, candles: candles.length, ...out }, null, 2));\n        return;\n      } catch (e) { /* tenta pr\u00f3ximo host */ }\n    }\n    console.error('falha ao obter klines (nenhum host alcan\u00e7\u00e1vel)');\n    process.exit(1);\n  })();\n}\n"
}
```

> === re-run backtest (cached) ===

> === Conclusoes (tail do relatorio) ===
> | 10/30 | -34.44% | 167.3 |
> | 20/50 | -25.46% | 92.8 |
> | 50/200 | -10.90% | 27.5 |
> 
> ## Conclusões (honestas)
> 
> 1. **Foi um ano de baixa**: Buy&Hold médio **-24.27%** nos 4 símbolos. Estratégias **long-only não escapam de bear market** — todas perderam no agregado. (Período adverso de propósito, p/ stress.)
> 2. **O maior edge é o estado de CAIXA**: onde o classificador detectou "bear" e saiu (ex.: BTC: regime-switch ~-7% vs B&H ~-27% OOS), cortou a perda pela metade+. Sair do mercado em baixa vale mais que escolher entre estratégias compradas.
> 3. **O classificador cru (ADX + SMA200) ainda é ruidoso**: regime-switching bate Buy&Hold em só **1/4** símbolos — ajuda muito em uns, atrapalha em outros por classificar mal a janela. O edge existe, mas depende da QUALIDADE do classificador.
> 4. **Menos trades perderam menos**: RSI 25/75 (≈17 trades) perdeu bem menos que 35/65 (≈53); SMA 50/200 menos que 10/30. Fee + whipsaw punem o overtrading — confirma que HFT/alta-frequência é matematicamente desfavorável (move típico < round-trip de fee).
> 5. **Próximos passos**: (a) classificador melhor — multi-timeframe, histerese anti-whipsaw, +DI/-DI além do ADX; (b) formalizar o filtro macro "risk-off" (o lever de caixa); (c) rodar numa janela de ALTA (ex.: 2023-2024) p/ ver o outro lado do ciclo.
> 
> ---

No **bull** (2023-10→2024-04, BTC $27k→$73k): **tudo ganhou, mas Buy&Hold ESMAGOU** o trading ativo. B&H médio **+294%** vs regime-switch +39%, trend +147%, MR +67%. Regime-switch bate B&H em **0/4** no bull.

A conclusão completa das duas pontas do ciclo é a verdade dura da validação:

- O valor real do trading ativo é **risco-ajustado** (menor drawdown, sair na baixa), **não** outperformance.
- O classificador cru sai cedo/whipsaw no bull → precisa de histerese.

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/-home-gcrux-tradeux/memory/git-ownership-gotcha.md",
  "content": "---\nname: git-ownership-gotcha\ndescription: On the TradeUX box run ALL git/docker repo ops as gcrux-tradeux \u2014 git-as-ubuntu poisons .git ownership and blocks commits\nmetadata:\n  type: project\n---\n\nThe repo `/home/gcrux-tradeux/tradeux` is owned by `gcrux-tradeux`, but the interactive shell runs as `ubuntu`.\n\nRunning git as `ubuntu` \u2014 even read-only `git -C ... -c safe.directory='*' log/branch/fetch` \u2014 creates `ubuntu`-owned files inside `.git` (e.g. `.git/config`, `.git/refs/remotes/origin/...`). After that, `gcrux-tradeux` can no longer write `.git/objects`, so the next commit fails with:\n`error: insufficient permission for adding an object to repository database .git/objects`.\n\n**Fix:** `sudo -n chown -R gcrux-tradeux:gcrux-tradeux /home/gcrux-tradeux/tradeux/.git` (only `ubuntu` has passwordless sudo; `gcrux-tradeux` does NOT \u2014 so chown must run from the ubuntu shell, not inside a `sudo -u gcrux-tradeux` script).\n\n**Rule:** do ALL git (commit/push/fetch) and any docker run that writes into the repo as `sudo -n -u gcrux-tradeux`. For containers that write outputs into the repo, pass `--user \"$(id -u):$(id -g)\"` (run as gcrux uid) so outputs are gcrux-owned and no chown is needed. Push over HTTPS with the token from `.env` (`GITHUB_TOKEN_API`) inline in the URL \u2014 never persist it in `.git/config`.\n\nSee [[tradeux-orchestration-model]] and [[github-ssh-key-on-ubuntu]].\n"
}
```

## 🟢 Sessão completa — dois tracks entregues, em produção, documentados

### Track A — Segurança ✅
- **Dependabot: 28 → 6** alertas. **1 critical + 8 high → 0 / 0** (confirmado pela API do GitHub).
- Correção: **twilio 3→6** (mata as 8 highs transitivas) + **`overrides` form-data** (mata o critical), com **gate QA + Tech-Lead aprovado**, backend **recriado em prod** (boot limpo, baseline re-armada), commit `d1b3b47`. `SECURITY.md` atualizado.
- Restam **6 moderate** transitivas via `node-binance-api@0.13` → **deferidas** pra uma task própria (bump do core = hot-path; destrava **OCO**).

### Track B — Backtest robusto + detector de regime ✅
- **Harness walk-forward** ([backend/scripts/backtest.js](backend/scripts/backtest.js)): dados reais Binance 1h, fee-adjusted, sem lookahead, OOS, sensibilidade de params, regime-switching 3-estados.
- **Detector de regime** ([backend/src/utils/regimeClassifier.js](backend/src/utils/regimeClassifier.js)): ADX+SMA200, testado ao vivo (BTC→`bear/cash` hoje).
- Rodei **os dois lados do ciclo** (commits `e9df9b1`, `a0d2ddb`, síntese em [backtest-results/README.md](backend/backtest-results/README.md)):

| Janela | Buy&Hold | Regime-switch | Lição |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Bear** (365d) | -24% | -32% (BTC -7% vs -27%) | edge = **estado de CAIXA** (drawdown) |
| **Bull** (2024) | **+294%** | +39% | **B&H esmaga** trading ativo |

> **Verdade dura da validação:** Buy&Hold é dificílimo de bater em **retorno bruto**; o valor do trading ativo é **risco-ajustado** (menos drawdown), não outperformance.
